## `AFG-0121-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Maturity Analyst
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Wine Grape Sugar-Acid Ratio & Phenolic Maturity
